Before this, a fresh tap install exposed **zero** working knowledge commands: `install_kanban_helpers()` prefers `share/templates/aliases/kanban-aliases.sh` (which had 0 knowledge functions), and the fallback `share/templates/kanban/kanban-helpers.template.sh` carried only `kb-knowledge-search` (ported under XACA-0743) — so `kb-knowledge-add/promote/validate/reindex` were undefined for every tap consumer (M1Pro/M4Mini). Both shipped helpers now carry the complete subsystem, ported faithfully from the canonical dev-team `kanban-helpers.sh` (four-tier XACA-0222 schema). **(1) `share/templates/aliases/kanban-aliases.sh`** (the preferred installed file) — added all 15 functions: 5 public (`kb-knowledge-search`, `-add`, `-promote`, `-validate`, `-reindex`), 8 private (`_kb_knowledge_global_root`, `_kb_knowledge_slugify`, `_kb_knowledge_yaml_field`, `_kb_knowledge_project_path`, `_kb_knowledge_project_display_name`, `_kb_knowledge_tier_rank`, `_kb_knowledge_resolve_ref`, `_kb_knowledge_reindex_one`), and 2 validators (`_kb_validate_name_component`, `_kb_validate_subject_path`). Context-adapted for the aliases file's "works without tmux/dev-team context" contract: the single `_kb_detect_context` call (in `kb-knowledge-search`'s telemetry persona-resolution) was swapped to this file's `_kb_get_team`, and a hardcoded `$HOME/dev-team/kanban-logs` telemetry path was changed to `${AITEAMFORGE_DIR}/kanban-logs` so a bare tap machine never spawns a stray `~/dev-team` directory. **(2) `share/templates/kanban/kanban-helpers.template.sh`** (fallback) — added the 7-function gap (`kb-knowledge-add`, `-promote`, `-validate`, `-reindex` + `_kb_knowledge_tier_rank`, `_kb_knowledge_resolve_ref`, `_kb_knowledge_reindex_one`); the pre-existing `kb-knowledge-search`, 5 privates, both validators, and `_kb_detect_context` were already present and were not duplicated. Also fixed the same `$HOME/dev-team/kanban-logs` → `${AITEAMFORGE_DIR}/kanban-logs` telemetry-path coupling in this file's pre-existing `kb-knowledge-search` — this resolves the **XACA-0743 review finding #4 (telemetry log path parity)** that was deferred to a follow-up. **(3) `tests/test-xaca-0746-kb-knowledge-in-tap.sh`** — new sandboxed fresh-install suite (12 cases, `TEST_TMP_DIR`-isolated, no dev-team checkout assumptions): asserts all 15 functions are defined in both shipped files, exercises an `add`→`search`→`validate`→`reindex`→`promote(dry-run)` round trip against a temp `~/knowledge` root, structurally asserts the ported aliases block has no tmux/`$HOME/dev-team` coupling, and verifies graceful degradation when `git` is absent. **(4) `tests/ci-manifest`** — registered `test-xaca-0746-…` and back-filled the missing `test-xaca-0743-four-tier-knowledge.sh` entry (a pre-existing manifest gap) as `plain-shell`. Note: `kb-knowledge-validate` is pure shell/awk (no `python3` dependency); the only external tool the subsystem touches is `git` (in `_kb_knowledge_project_path`), which already falls back to `projects/unknown` when absent. These two template files are the tap-side source of truth for these functions (canonical `kanban-helpers.sh` is dev-only / not tap-mirrored, XACA-0632). Foundational step of EPIC-0047 (knowledge system fleet-wide); does not itself cut a release (XACA-0751) or provision `~/knowledge` (XACA-0747, already partially shipped via XACA-0743's `install_knowledge_tree`).

### Changed
- XACA-0748 (EPIC-0047) — `share/scripts/kb-init-team`: reworked the knowledge-scaffolding site (`_create_dir_tree`) to provision a newly-provisioned team's slots in the four-tier knowledge schema (XACA-0222 / `~/knowledge/SPEC.md`), replacing the dead pre-XACA-0222 per-avatar + `knowledge/TEAM/` repo-local layout. New layout: project tier in-repo at `<kanban-dir>/knowledge/project/INDEX.md` (SPEC §4.4) + `retrospectives/`; reserved team tier + create-if-missing agent tier under `$KB_KNOWLEDGE_GLOBAL_ROOT` (default `~/knowledge`, best-effort — warn+skip when absent, never a hard failure); and a `.knowledge-config.yml` discovery anchor written at the team's working dir so the in-repo project tier resolves for current-project searches (any team, container-layout included). Idempotent; never clobbers an existing globally-merged agent INDEX. Consumer-relevant because on a tap install `kb-init-team` runs in LOCAL-ONLY mode and provisions the board + this knowledge tree; pairs with the XACA-0743 installer that ships `~/knowledge/`. Mirror of the canonical `dev-team/scripts/kb-init-team` (canonical-source rule XACA-0340).

## [0.16.1] - 2026-07-01

### Fixed
- XACA-0752 — `share/scripts/kb-tap-release` line ~206 `mktemp` template fixed to a portable trailing-X form. On macOS/BSD, `mktemp` only randomizes TRAILING X's — the previous `mktemp /tmp/kb-tap-release-footer.XXXXXX.py` template put a `.py` suffix AFTER the X's, so mktemp created a LITERAL, non-random file (`python3` runs the script fine regardless of extension, so the suffix was cosmetic-only). A SIGKILL'd run (the `trap '...' EXIT INT TERM` cleanup never fires on SIGKILL) could leave that literal file behind, and the next release-cut's `mktemp` then failed at Phase 1 with `mkstemp failed ... File exists` — hit live during the v0.16.0 cut, requiring manual `/tmp` cleanup. Fix: `FOOTER_SCRIPT=$(mktemp "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/kb-tap-release-footer.XXXXXX")` (trailing-X only, `TMPDIR` fallback) plus an idempotent `rm -f /tmp/kb-tap-release-footer.XXXXXX.py` immediately before the `mktemp` call so an already-poisoned machine self-heals on the next run. The `trap 'rm -f "$FOOTER_SCRIPT"' EXIT INT TERM` cleanup is unchanged. Mirror of the canonical dev-team change (canonical-source rule XACA-0340).

## [0.16.0] - 2026-06-30

### Changed
- XACA-0743 — `share/templates/kanban/kanban-helpers.template.sh` `kb-knowledge-search` migrated from the pre-XACA-0222 two-tier model (per-team `<repo>/kanban/knowledge` directories, `--team`/`--tag` filtering only) to the four-tier `~/knowledge/{agents,subjects,teams}` + project-tier schema (XACA-0222). Adds `--agent`, `--subject`, `--project`, `--tier`, and `--all-projects` flags (`--team` preserved as a backward-compat alias for `--agent`), multi-term OR matching on id/title (XACA-0738), and porcelain/JSON machine-readable output. `libexec/installers/install-kanban.sh` now provisions and migrates the four-tier `~/knowledge/` tree for tap consumers (previously only the dev-team installer did this). The function body is ported faithfully from the canonical dev-team `kb-knowledge-search` (XACA-0222/XACA-0738) to keep behavior identical; this template is the tap-side source of truth for the function (the canonical `kanban-helpers.sh` is dev-only / not tap-mirrored per XACA-0632). Review fold-in (PR #661): `migrate_legacy_repo_knowledge` gains an empty-search-roots guard (avoids a macOS bash 3.2 empty-array-under-`set -u` crash when `KB_KNOWLEDGE_MIGRATE_SEARCH_ROOTS` is whitespace-only) and a slug-collision warning (same-basename repos merging into one `projects/<slug>/` now emit a warning; copy-not-move already preserves all sources); test suite extended to 19 cases.
- XACA-0684 (XACA-0338 F-03-018) — Standardized shebangs to the portable `#!/usr/bin/env <interpreter>` form across the mirrored shell scripts in `share/` (55 files: per-team `share/scripts/teams/**` startup/shutdown/banner scripts plus the shared tooling mirrored via sync-tap.sh). Every `#!/bin/{bash,zsh,sh}` became the matching `#!/usr/bin/env` form; interpreter family is never changed (line-1-only swap, verified per-file). Mirror of the canonical dev-team change via sync-tap.sh (canonical-source rule XACA-0340). Rationale: env-form is the only shebang that works for every script — some use bash 4+ features impossible under macOS `/bin/bash` 3.2, and env-bash resolves to Homebrew bash 5.3.

### Added
- XACA-0688 (F-09-016) — `share/lcars-ui/integrations/sync_service.py` + provider files gain kanban→external bidirectional sync. Adds `IntegrationProvider.update_ticket()` (JIRA via PUT/transitions, Monday.com via GraphQL mutations, GitHub Issues via PATCH; default "not supported"), `SyncService._sync_kanban_to_external()` + `_get_external_status()` reverse status map, and wires the previously-stubbed `KANBAN_TO_EXTERNAL`/`BIDIRECTIONAL` directions in `sync_ticket_link()`. Resolves audit finding F-09-016. Mirrored from canonical `lcars-ui/integrations/` (XACA-0340).
- XACA-0689 — `share/lcars-ui/calendar/sync_service.py` + `share/lcars-ui/calendar/test_sync_service.py` — mirror of the canonical court-date calendar event sync implementation (XACA-0340; audit F-09-016). Replaces the prior `# TODO: Implement separate court date event sync` stub with a full court-date event lifecycle (create / update / orphan-delete) in a new `_sync_court_date_event` helper. An epic's `metadata.courtDate` is synced as its own `⚖️ COURT:` calendar event with state tracked separately under `metadata.courtDateSync`, independent of the epic's own due-date event. Adds 5 unit tests covering create/update/orphan-delete/independence/no-court. Test+runtime mirror; no consumer-facing behavior change beyond the calendar sync feature itself.
- XACA-0728 — `aiteamforge doctor` gains a `lcars-python-runtime` check (exposed as `--check lcars-python-runtime`, wired into the `all` pass and the silent first-launch preflight subset) that stands guard against the XACA-0713 class of crash. Two surfaces neither `check_python_venv` (validates venv packages via the login-shell `AITEAMFORGE_PYTHON`) nor any other check covered: (1) **server.py 3.9 import-safety** — `py_compile`s the shipped `share/lcars-ui/server.py` under macOS system `/usr/bin/python3`, FAILing if the PEP-604 annotations are not deferred (the original `int | None` TypeError); (2) **launchd-context resolver** — resolves the interpreter exactly as the `com.aiteamforge.lcars-health` daemon would (scrubbed `PATH` with no brew, `HOME` preserved, no `LCARS_PYTHON` override) via `resolve_lcars_python`, FAILing if it returns system `python3` or an interpreter `<3.10`. Remediations point at `brew postinstall aiteamforge` / `brew upgrade aiteamforge`. Tap-native change (`libexec/commands/aiteamforge-doctor.sh`); no canonical-source mirror.

Updated the function name and the semantic verb so a future rename of kb-init-team follows the correct sibling trail. Comment-only change; no behavior impact. Tap-native (no canonical-source mirror). Fast-follow from the XACA-0698 PR #638 review.
- XACA-0675 — `share/templates/kanban/kanban-helpers.template.sh` — backport of the XACA-0665 `kb-done` hardening to the shipped template (the template is an older fork that never received it). (1) **Write-guard:** both `_kb_jq_update` calls in `kb-done` (subitem-completion and main-item-completion paths) are now wrapped in `if ! _kb_jq_update ...; then echo "board write failed" >&2; return 1; fi`, so `item_found=true` and the `"Task completed!"` message are unreachable when the board write aborts. (2) **Date hardening:** all four `TZ=UTC date -j -f "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S" "${work_started_at%Z}"` time-computation sites now strip fractional seconds via nested zsh expansion `${${work_started_at%\.[0-9]*}%Z}` before parsing, so pre-existing microsecond timestamps no longer break the `date -j -f` parse. The `fromdateiso8601` jq sites and the `.000Z` writer from XACA-0665 do not exist in this older template fork, so they are not applicable. Tap-only file; no canonical-source mirror.
- XACA-0729 — `share/templates/kanban/board-template.json` now seeds a new board's `releaseConfig.defaultEnvironments` with the PLANNED-leading `["PLANNED","DEV","QA","ALPHA","BETA","GAMMA","PROD"]` and adds `"PLANNED":{"enabled":true,"required":true}` as the first `flowConfig.stages` entry, matching `DEFAULT_RELEASE_CONFIG` in the LCARS server. Previously the template omitted the leading `PLANNED` stage, so freshly-provisioned teams birthed releases ACTIVE (seeded at `DEV`) instead of in the PLANNED holding state. Tap-native template (no canonical-source mirror in dev-team); the dev-team-side `kb-init-team`/`kb-freelance` seeding hardening and `lcars-ui/server.py` runtime defense ship alongside in the outer repo.
- XACA-0698 — Three consumer fresh-install gaps surfaced by the XACA-0653 E2E gate (CI run `ddc4580`, 3 unexpected doctor failures). (1) **kb-init-team absent on no-team fresh install** (`libexec/installers/install-kanban.sh`): new `install_kb_init_team_scripts()` — mirrors the `install_kb_port_reconcile_script` pattern — copies `share/scripts/kb-init-team-guard.sh` and `share/scripts/kb-init-team` to `$AITEAMFORGE_DIR/scripts/` unconditionally; called from `install_kanban_system()` alongside the other shared tools. Previously both files were installed ONLY by `install-team.sh` which runs per-team — so a fresh install with no team selected left kb-init-team absent, causing E2E step 3 ("cannot provision a team") to hard-FAIL, cascading steps 4-6. **Upgrade path** (`libexec/commands/aiteamforge-upgrade.sh`): added `kb-init-team-guard.sh` and `kb-init-team` to `_xaca0673_mandatory_materialize_basenames()` so `update_runtime_helpers` materializes them even when absent (same pattern as `iterm2_venv_bootstrap.py`). (2) **`{{AITEAMFORGE_DIR}}` literal in template fallback** (`share/templates/aliases/kanban-aliases.sh`, `share/templates/kanban/kanban-helpers.template.sh`): removed the `{{AITEAMFORGE_DIR}}` placeholder from all `${AITEAMFORGE_DIR:-{{AITEAMFORGE_DIR}}}` fallback expressions (changed to bare `${AITEAMFORGE_DIR}`). After the installer's sed substitution, the template is rendered correctly — but if someone sourced the raw template without rendering it, the `:-{{...}}` fallback emitted a literal placeholder string as the path. The test harness (step 6) already guarded against this with a `grep -q '{{'` rejection, but removing the embedded placeholder from the template eliminates the root cause. (3) **E2E doctor allowlist cascade protection** (`tests/test-xaca-0653-e2e-fresh-install.sh`): added "Org identity is the unconfigured placeholder" to the Step 7 sandbox-artifact allowlist. When kb-init-team was absent and step 3 failed, `team-paths.json` was never written, the doctor's `check_kanban_health` reported the `example-org` placeholder failure — an UNEXPECTED failure not previously in the allowlist — causing a second gate failure cascading from the primary kb-init-team defect. Fix (1) resolves the root cause; this allowlist addition is defence-in-depth. (4) **`aiteamforge doctor` reporting iTerm2/Claude Code as hard failures in CI** (`libexec/commands/aiteamforge-doctor.sh`): `check_dependencies()` now detects CI context (`GITHUB_ACTIONS=true` or `CI=true`) and downgrades iTerm2/Claude Code "not found" from `fail` to `warn` (expected-absent on headless GH Actions runners); on real consumer workstations neither env var is set, so these remain hard failures that alert the user to a misconfigured install.
- XACA-0681 — `share/lcars-ui/tests/test_ccusage_collector.py` — mirror of the canonical test fix (XACA-0340). Four pre-existing red tests asserted the ccusage collector still emitted `schema_version == 2` while `ccusage_collector.py` ships `SCHEMA_VERSION = 4`; the assertions, the four `*_is_2*` test-method names, and the `CollectWeeklySuccessTests` class docstring are bumped to 4. The `CollectRollingFailureTests.setUp` `prev_cache` fixture intentionally stays at `"schema_version": 2` (an input old-cache that exercises the v2→v4 migration/weekly-preservation path; the output cache is overwritten with `SCHEMA_VERSION` and asserted = 4). Test-only mirror; no shipped runtime behavior change.

## [0.15.1] - 2026-06-16

### Added
- test: XACA-0707 — Add tap test-manifest completeness CI gate. New `tests/ci-manifest` classifies every `tests/test-*.sh` into one of four categories: `plain-shell` (runs under stock bash on macos-latest; no special deps or real install), `brew-bash` (requires Homebrew bash 4+; declared-A associative arrays), `real-install` (requires real `brew install aiteamforge`; post-install verification), and `excluded:<reason>` (never auto-run; reason mandatory). New `tests/ci-manifest-check.sh` gate (run in CI job `manifest-completeness-guard`) FAILS if any `tests/test-*.sh` file is missing from the manifest, if the manifest lists a removed file, or on malformed category/excluded-without-reason. `.github/workflows/tests.yml` `test-shell-homebrew-tap` job now uses the manifest to auto-enroll all `plain-shell` tests (manifest-driven loop: `awk '$2=="plain-shell"' tests/ci-manifest | while read f; do bash tests/test-runner.sh "tests/$f"; done`) — no YAML edits needed to wire a new plain-shell test. Brew-bash/real-install/excluded special-cases preserved in separate YAML steps. Anti-drift: each new test must be added to the manifest or CI blocks the merge. Comprehensive decision guide added to `CONTRIBUTING.md` § "Adding a tap test — the CI manifest". Follow-up review fixes: manifest-driven plain-shell loop now skips `test-xaca-0653-runner-exit-propagation.sh` (already run as the dedicated pre-loop runner-integrity meta-test, preventing a double-run); `CONTRIBUTING.md` illustrative loop snippet updated to reflect the real loop's error-handling (`set -euo pipefail`, per-test FAILED accumulation, non-zero exit on any failure).
- test: XACA-0704 — Wire upgrade acceptance gate as CI release gate. Adds two shell test suites to `.github/workflows/tests.yml` (`test-shell-homebrew-tap` job): `test-xaca-0704-positive.sh` (16 assertions — positive upgrade path: box one version behind advances to next version, verified via brew list --versions, .installed-version stamp, and get_installed_version()) and `test-xaca-0704-negative.sh` (15 assertions — negative path: stuck/no-op upgrade exits 1 + FAILURE banner; refused-tap upgrade exits 1 + FAILURE banner; current box under --non-interactive exits 0 with no false failure). Both suites use a mocked `brew` PATH-shim — no real tap install required; run on `macos-latest`. Tests live only in the tap (`homebrew-tap/tests/`) with no canonical-source mirror in dev-team (tap-only files, per sync-tap.sh path list).

### Fixed
- XACA-0713 — LCARS server no longer crash-loops on tap consumers whose macOS system `python3` is 3.9 (cockpit "unable to load" fleet-wide on 0.14.0). Three folded fixes, all mirrored from canonical via sync-tap.sh (XACA-0340): (1) **share/lcars-ui/server.py** — adds `from __future__ import annotations` so PEP-604 `int | None` annotations (e.g. `_is_cache_stale`) are not eagerly evaluated at import/def-time; under macOS system python 3.9.6 the eager eval raised `TypeError: unsupported operand type(s) for |: 'type' and 'NoneType'`, hard-crashing the server on start. Audited for other 3.10+ runtime constructs (match/case, runtime unions, get_type_hints/cast) — none beyond the deferred annotations. (2) **share/scripts/lcars-launch-helpers.sh** — `resolve_lcars_python()` now probes the known ABSOLUTE venv paths directly (`/opt/homebrew/var/aiteamforge/venv`, Intel `/usr/local/...`, cellar `libexec/venv`) and locates `brew` by absolute path when off-PATH, BEFORE any `brew --prefix` dependence. Previously a non-login/launchd context (the `com.aiteamforge.lcars-health` daemon) had no `/opt/homebrew/bin` on PATH → `brew --prefix` returned empty → the venv probe built a prefix-less path → fell through to bare system `python3` (3.9) → server crashed. Now non-login + daemon launches resolve the venv (3.14), never system 3.9. (3) **share/scripts/lcars-health-check.sh** — `_hc_start_lcars_server` emits a LOUD warning when the resolved interpreter is <3.10, so a crash-loop/SIGTERM-race can never silently kill the cockpit.
- XACA-0706 — `aiteamforge doctor` now detects a running LCARS server bound to a NON-canonical port (root cause of XACA-0613). New `check_lcars_port_drift` check (exposed as `--check lcars-port-drift`, wired into the `all` pass): sweeps live `server.py` processes via `ps eww`, reads each server's team from its `LCARS_TEAM=` env and bound port from the `server.py <PORT>` argv, then compares against the team's canonical port from the shipped `share/kanban-hooks/lcars_ports.py` resolver. A mismatch is a FAIL with an actionable remediation (run `lcars-health-check.sh` — which now self-heals — or `aiteamforge restart kanban`). Companion to the canonical `share/scripts/lcars-health-check.sh` change (mirrored via sync-tap.sh) that detects and surgically self-heals the same drift: it kills only the affected `<instance>-lcars` tmux session + the stale wrong-port `server.py` and recreates on the canonical port, never re-running the team's full startup script.
- XACA-0705-003 — `lcars-ui/server.py _build_team_kanban_dirs()` no longer hardcodes `has_schema=True` in its call to `config_is_structurally_valid()`. Previously the hardcoded value was correct (list_teams() enforces schema_version via load_config()) but invisible — a future relaxation of that invariant would silently pass schema-less configs. Fix: derive `_has_schema` from a live `_aiteamforge_load_config()` call (`"schema_version" in (cfg or {})`), with a fallback to `True` on exception so load_config failures remain handled by the outer except block. No behavior change for healthy configs. (Mirrored from canonical `lcars-ui/server.py` via sync-tap.sh; XACA-0340.)
- XACA-0705 — `kanban-hooks/aiteamforge_paths.py` (`load_config()` schema-integrity guard) no longer treats a consumer config with no academy team as corrupt. A config is now valid iff `schema_version` is present AND at least one non-academy team exists — academy absence is diagnostic-only, not a corruption signal. Adds `config_is_structurally_valid()` as the shared predicate used by both `load_config()` and the LCARS server's `_build_team_kanban_dirs()`, so the validity rule cannot drift between the two sites. Corruption signatures unchanged: missing schema_version, empty teams, academy-alone. Regression tests added. (Mirrored from canonical `kanban-hooks/aiteamforge_paths.py` via sync-tap.sh; XACA-0340.)
- XACA-0702 — `aiteamforge upgrade` no longer reports a false "Dev-team has been upgraded successfully!" + exit 0 when Homebrew never advanced. Four folded fixes: (1) **upgrade.sh** — `check_brew_updates()` now snapshots the installed brew version (`brew list --versions aiteamforge`) before the brew step and detects a no-op: it sets a module-level `UPGRADE_BREW_FAILED` flag when the tap is still refused after `ensure_tap_trusted` (untrusted/trust-gated Homebrew), when an interactive `brew upgrade` left the installed version unchanged, or when `--non-interactive` finds the formula STILL `brew outdated` after the caller's brew run. The final summary prints a LOUD boxed FAILURE with the `brew trust --tap <tap>` remediation and `exit 1` instead of faking success. The `.installed-version` stamp now records the REAL `brew list --versions` value when brew is the install method (not blindly the framework VERSION), so `doctor`/`status` drift detection is not fooled into thinking the box advanced. (2) **config.sh** — `get_installed_version()` now prefers the freshest accurate source (live brew version → `.installed-version` stamp → legacy config `version`) so `aiteamforge status` stops showing a stale install-time version after a `brew upgrade`. (3) **share/lcars-ui/ccusage_collector.py** — `find_ccusage()` adds an `npx -y ccusage` fallback (XACA-0243 class) when neither PATH nor fnm globs resolve; now returns a command-prefix LIST splatted into argv at every call site so the multi-token npx prefix is preserved. Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/ccusage_collector.py` via sync-tap.sh. (4) **start.sh** — `check_port_health()` (covers `start` and `restart`) now AUTO-APPLIES the documented port reconcile (`aiteamforge-port-fix --apply`) and re-verifies instead of aborting with a "fix it yourself" message; only fails if the port collision persists after the reconcile. Graceful-degrade (tool absent → warn + continue) preserved.
- XACA-0653 — E2E fresh-install gate driver (`tests/test-xaca-0653-e2e-fresh-install.sh`)
  now passes on a real tap-installed consumer (verified ×3 on a v0.15.0 box). The
  shipped v0.15.0 driver failed every downstream step on a real install, so the
  durability crux (the XACA-0652 regression this gate exists to catch) had never
  actually executed. Fixes: (1) probe the real installed layout — `kb-init-team`,
  `server.py`, and `lcars-launch-helpers.sh` ship under `libexec/share/…`, not
  `libexec/…`; (2) render the consumer resolver into the sandbox the way
  `install_kanban_helpers` does (the install tree ships only templates) and refuse
  to source an unrendered template; (3) register the throwaway team in
  `team-paths.json` ourselves — tap-install `kb-init-team` runs in local-only mode
  and deliberately skips all "product-managed" registration sites; (4) export
  `LCARS_UI_DIR` so the product launch helper `cd`s into the installed UI; (5)
  re-assert the team in `team-paths.json` before the resolver check, because LCARS
  server startup regenerates the per-machine overlay from `DEFAULT_TEAMS` and
  discards non-default entries; (6) scope the `doctor` assertion to tolerate
  sandbox-only artifacts (absent academy board, unrendered health-check script)
  while still failing on any real dependency regression.

### Added
- XACA-0703: add Academy UX evaluator persona (lal) to `share/personas/academy/agents/academy_lal_ux_persona.md`.

## [0.15.0] - 2026-06-15

### Fixed
- XACA-0701 — `scripts/check-tap-hygiene.sh`: version-line grep no longer aborts under `set -eo pipefail` when Formula has no version line (XACA-0394 regression); Formula version line now optional, tag must match VERSION strictly.
- XACA-0700: `share/scripts/kb-tap-release` — guard grep calls against no-match abort under `set -euo pipefail`. XACA-0394 removed the standalone `version` line from `Formula/aiteamforge.rb`; two sites in kb-tap-release still grep for it, causing silent set-e death and blocking all releases. Fix: `|| true` on both grep command substitutions; Check 11 treats absent version line as valid; Phase 1 post-edit verify skips version check when line is absent. Tag verification remains strict.

### Added
- XACA-0697: Mirror canonical `lcars-ui/js/lcars-cr-tab.js` + `lcars-ui/index.html` — the RELEASE column in the CHANGE REQUESTS pipeline list now renders the assigned release as `shortTitle - name` (Label + Title), matching the Backlog REL: filter dropdown. `shortTitle` is resolved live from `boardData.releases`/`archivedReleases` by `releaseId` (no board-schema change, no backfill). Cache-buster `lcars-cr-tab.js?v=1.1` → `?v=1.2`. Mirror via sync-tap.sh of canonical dev-team changes.

- test: XACA-0653 — Tap-native CI test infrastructure. Adds an E2E fresh-install gate (`tests/test-xaca-0653-e2e-fresh-install.sh`) that installs the formula from the local tap and drives the runner end-to-end, plus a runner exit-propagation regression (`tests/test-xaca-0653-runner-exit-propagation.sh`) covering the case where a suite exits non-zero with no `FAIL:` lines. Hardens `tests/test-runner.sh` to count an exit-code failure when `fails==0` (previously such a hard failure was reported as "All tests passed!", silently green-lighting a broken gate) and wires both as CI jobs in `.github/workflows/tests.yml`.

- XACA-0690: `share/scripts/iterm2_venv_bootstrap.py` now probes the repo-local `.venv` as a dev-box fallback (candidate 5) when no tap-owned venv is found. Resolves `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'iterm2'` on the M3Pro dev source machine after a homebrew python relink — the tap is never installed on that machine so only the repo dev venv has iterm2. Worktree-aware: if `<repo_root>/.git` is a file (git worktree pointer) the gitdir path is parsed to resolve the main-checkout root. Consumer machines are unaffected — no repo `.venv` exists in the tap layout, so the new candidate is harmlessly skipped. Mirror of canonical `dev-team/scripts/iterm2_venv_bootstrap.py` via sync-tap.sh. (XACA-0690-002)

- XACA-0394: Audit Batch E tech debt (F-09-016) — add LIMITATION docstrings to two sync stubs: `share/lcars-ui/integrations/sync_service.py` (`sync_ticket_link`) documents that kanban→external bidirectional sync is not implemented (tracked XACA-0688); `share/lcars-ui/calendar/sync_service.py` (`_sync_outbound_single`) documents that court-date calendar sync is silently skipped (tracked XACA-0689). Both TODO comments annotated with tracking ticket IDs.

- XACA-0620: TimePad team-aware hook dispatcher — mirror canonical into the tap. New `share/kanban-hooks/timepad-track.sh` (thin SessionStart/SessionEnd wrapper: gathers git branch + repo toplevel, sources `~/.timepad-secrets`, hands off to the dispatcher), `share/kanban-hooks/timepad-track.py` (team-aware dispatcher: resolves the owning team by longest-prefix match against `get_team_working_dir()`, gates on `is_timepad_enabled()`, loads config via `get_timepad_team_config()`, resolves token via the vault-primary + env-failover chain, resolves the kanban item from the branch against the team board, applies the configured `tagId`; API base from config `apiBaseUrl` — no hardcoded host; fail-soft `exit 0`, idempotent start, 8h stale cap), and `share/kanban-hooks/timepad_tags.py` (name-only category classifier; retired-host tag UUIDs removed). Relocated from the unversioned `~/.config/timeapp/`; hard rebrand — no legacy-host reference. Disabled for all teams by default (lands dormant). Includes the dispatcher unit-test mirror `share/kanban-hooks/test_timepad_track_0620.py` (10 cases: longest-prefix team resolution, enable-gate short-circuit, placeholder no-op, padding-insensitive item resolution, classifier).

- XACA-0394: Formula `brew audit --strict` fix (F-07-007) — remove redundant `version "0.14.0"` declaration from `Formula/aiteamforge.rb`. The version is already encoded in the `url` tag (`v0.14.0`); a standalone `version` line is flagged as redundant by Homebrew's `FormulaAudit` cop. `brew style` confirms no offenses after removal.

- XACA-0619: TimePad per-team config foundation — mirror canonical into the tap. New `share/kanban-hooks/timepad_config.json` (per-team schema, 11 teams; `apiBaseUrl` default `https://timepad.io/api`; `tokenRef` name-only reference; UUIDs ship as `<fetch-from-timepad.io>` placeholders) and `share/kanban-hooks/timepad_config.py` (token resolution on the vault-primary + env-failover chain, masked-fingerprint accessor, `_skip_vault` kwarg for hermetic tests). `share/kanban-hooks/aiteamforge_paths.py` gains the loader/validate/cache + accessor API (`load_timepad_config`, `is_timepad_enabled`, `get_timepad_team_config`, etc.). `share/lcars-ui/server.py` gains the `teamConfig.timepadSupport` enable toggle via `/api/team-config` (mirrors `crSupport`; support-block `description` capped at 500 chars). Test mirrors `share/kanban-hooks/test_timepad_0619.py` + `test_accessor_005.py`. Schema/secret/gate only — no hook/UI behavior. Hard rebrand: no legacy-host reference.

- XACA-0394: Shell hygiene — Batch A. Add `-r` flag to all interactive `read` invocations in `bin/aiteamforge-setup.sh` (prevents backslash interpretation in user input); add ShellCheck disable directive + template comment to `share/templates/fleet-monitor/tailscale-funnel.template.sh` (SC1054/1073/1056/1072 false-positives from {{PLACEHOLDER}} tokens). Findings F-03-011 and F-03-014 from XACA-0338 audit.

- XACA-0387 (audit F-04-008): cap POST Content-Length (50 MiB → HTTP 413) and add 30s socket timeout on the LCARS server, hardening do_POST against memory-exhaustion and slow-loris DoS.

- XACA-0683: Guard the **remaining** bare `launchctl` load/unload calls in the installer + lifecycle scripts behind `AITEAMFORGE_SKIP_LAUNCHCTL=1` (follow-up to XACA-0682, which covered `install-fleet-monitor.sh` + `validate-install.sh`). The `_aitf_launchctl()` wrapper — which returns 0 without touching the GUI domain when `AITEAMFORGE_SKIP_LAUNCHCTL=1` — now has a single canonical home in `libexec/lib/common.sh` (the two XACA-0682 inline copies in `install-fleet-monitor.sh` and `validate-install.sh` were removed; `validate-install.sh`, a standalone-sourceable lib, now sources `common.sh` to obtain the wrapper). All executed mutating `launchctl load/unload` calls were routed through the wrapper across: `libexec/installers/install-kanban.sh` (23 sites — backup, lcars-health, lcars-watch, runatload, cellar-watch, CR-confluence-poller, auto-upgrade, legacy/teardown unloads), `bin/aiteamforge-setup.sh` (4 — uninstall + dev re-load loop), `libexec/commands/aiteamforge-start.sh` (2), `aiteamforge-stop.sh` (1), `aiteamforge-upgrade.sh` (2), `aiteamforge-migrate.sh` (2), `aiteamforge-uninstall.sh` (1), and the one auto-fix `launchctl load` in `aiteamforge-doctor.sh`. `setup.sh` now sources `common.sh` early (right after `AITEAMFORGE_HOME` resolution) so the wrapper is defined for the inline `uninstall` block, which runs and `exit 0`s before the later source. Read-only `launchctl list | grep` and user-facing "Run: launchctl load …" suggestion strings are intentionally left bare. New regression test `tests/test-xaca-0683-skip-launchctl.sh` (6 cases) asserts: exactly one wrapper definition (in `common.sh`), skip-vs-pass-through behavior (only the exact value `1` skips), no executed bare mutating `launchctl` survives in any guarded script (comment/quoted-string-aware static guard — proven non-vacuous against an injected offender), and that `validate-install.sh` resolves the wrapper when sourced standalone. Existing suites green: `test-validate-install` 79/0, `test-lifecycle` 22/22, `test-tailscale` (XACA-0682's wrapper test) all pass after convergence, `test-xaca-0510-launchagent-render` 15/15.
- XACA-0682: Test isolation fix for `tests/test-tailscale.sh` — the test was leaking a real `com.aiteamforge.tailscale-funnel.plist` into the developer's `~/Library/LaunchAgents` and bootstrapping a real launchd job on every run (XACA-0212 class). **Fixes:** (1) `$HOME` is now sandboxed to `$TEST_TMP_DIR/home` before any installer code runs, so all plist writes land in the sandbox; `REAL_HOME` is captured first for the isolation assertion. (2) A record-only `launchctl` mock is placed at the front of `PATH` — it logs every call to `MOCK_LAUNCHCTL_LOG` and exits 0 without touching the real GUI domain. (3) `_aitf_launchctl()` wrapper added to `libexec/installers/install-fleet-monitor.sh` (all five `launchctl load/unload` sites: tailscale-funnel, fleet-monitor, fleet-reporter × 2, and both uninstall paths) and `libexec/lib/validate-install.sh` (`_val_check_launchagents` auto-fix load) — all real-domain side effects are no-ops when `AITEAMFORGE_SKIP_LAUNCHCTL=1`. Read-only `launchctl list | grep` calls are unchanged. (4) Defensive EXIT trap added to `test-tailscale.sh` using absolute `/bin/launchctl bootout` (bypasses PATH mock) to evict any job that leaked past the guards. (5) Self-policing isolation assertion (`Section 7`) explicitly FAILs the suite if any `com.aiteamforge.*` or `com.devteam.tailscale-funnel` plist is found in `REAL_HOME/Library/LaunchAgents` or if such a job is loaded in the real GUI domain. Three new Section 3b tests assert sandbox-write correctness and mock-record behavior, plus a test for the `AITEAMFORGE_SKIP_LAUNCHCTL=1` guard. Also adds a clarifying comment to `test-runner.sh` explaining the macOS `mktemp -t PREFIX` behavior.

- XACA-0385: Move the ccusage collector's runtime files out of the world-writable `/tmp` directory (`share/lcars-ui/ccusage_collector.py`, `share/lcars-ui/server.py`, new `share/lcars-ui/ccusage_paths.py`, `share/lcars-ui/ccusage_heuristics.py`). The collector wrote its cache (`lcars-ccusage-cache.json`), atomic-write staging file, PID lock, and (server-side) respawn log to fixed `/tmp/lcars-ccusage-*` paths, so any local user could pre-create, symlink-attack the atomic `os.replace`, or feed tampered usage data to the dashboard. All four files now live under `~/.aiteamforge/run/` (mode `0o700`, owner-only). New shared module `ccusage_paths.py` is the single source of truth for the four paths — both the producer (`ccusage_collector.py`) and consumer (`server.py`) import from it, so the literals are no longer duplicated (kills k501 sibling-drift). Its `ensure_runtime_dir()` creates the dir on first use and enforces three invariants — mode `0o700`, not a symlink, owned by the current uid — raising a loud `RuntimeError` rather than writing to an attacker-controlled path (and defensively re-`chmod`s a loose-but-owned dir, since `mkdir`'s `mode=` is ignored when the dir already exists). Collector hard-exits if the module is missing; server degrades gracefully and ensures the dir before opening its log on respawn. Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/` via `sync-tap.sh`. From XACA-0338 audit finding F-04-005.

- XACA-0386: Restrict LCARS export/import staging dirs to the owner (mode 0o700) in `share/lcars-ui/server.py`. The server staged team-transfer artifacts — including encrypted and transiently-decrypted secrets bundles — under shared world-traversable `/tmp` dirs (`/tmp/lcars-exports`, `/tmp/lcars-imports`, `/tmp/lcars-secrets-imports`) with a plain `mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)` that inherited a umask-derived (typically `0o755`) mode, so any local user on a shared host could read another user's exports/secrets. New `_ensure_private_dir(path)` helper does `mkdir(..., mode=0o700)` **plus an explicit `chmod(0o700)`** — the chmod is required because `mkdir`'s `mode=` is ignored when the dir already exists (the common case for these long-lived `/tmp` dirs), so a pre-existing world-readable dir would otherwise stay loose. Applied at all five creation sites (both `EXPORT_DIR` calls, import scratch dir + parent, secrets-import extract, import-upload staging, secrets-import upload `job_dir`). New regression test `share/lcars-ui/tests/team_transfer/test_xaca_0386_private_export_dirs.py` (incl. the pre-existing-loose-dir tighten case). Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/server.py` via `sync-tap.sh`. From XACA-0338 audit finding F-04-007.

- XACA-0380: Add zip path-traversal containment to kanban-import (`share/lcars-ui/server.py`). New `_assert_zip_entries_contained(names, dest_root)` helper rejects absolute-path entries and `..`-escape entries before any extraction, applied at both kanban-import sinks: the v2 manifest-driven write loop (validates manifest relpaths against `Path.home()`) and the legacy `extractall` (validates `zipf.namelist()` against the scratch dir). Mirrors the existing secrets-import containment guard (validate-all-first — no files written when a bad entry is present). New regression test `share/lcars-ui/tests/test_xaca0380_zip_traversal_guard.py`. Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/server.py` via `sync-tap.sh`. From XACA-0338 audit finding F-04-001.

- XACA-0381: Thread-safety fix mirrored into `share/lcars-ui/server.py` + `share/lcars-ui/tests/test_secrets_workers.py` (canonical `lcars-ui/` via `sync-tap.sh`). Adds two `threading.Lock()` guards (`_IMPORT_JOBS_LOCK`, `_SECRETS_JOBS_LOCK`) and eight accessor helpers around the `IMPORT_JOBS` / `SECRETS_IMPORT_JOBS` / `SECRETS_EXPORT_JOBS` job-registry dicts, which were mutated by the HTTP handler thread and daemon worker threads without locking. Fixes a `RuntimeError: dictionary changed size during iteration` in `_prune_old_secrets_jobs()` (it iterated/deleted while workers created entries) plus torn reads in the import status-poll handler. Audit finding F-04-003 (XACA-0338). Follow-ups: also locks the plain `EXPORT_JOBS` dict (`_EXPORT_JOBS_LOCK` + `_export_job_*` helpers + locked `_prune_old_export_jobs()`), and closes the `staged→applying` check-then-act TOCTOU in both apply handlers via atomic compare-and-set helpers (losing concurrent caller gets HTTP 409, no duplicate worker thread).

- XACA-0382: Fleet Monitor production install now uses `npm ci --omit=dev` instead of `npm install --production` (`libexec/installers/install-fleet-monitor.sh`). `npm install` may mutate `package-lock.json` and resolve newer transitive versions at install time, bypassing lockfile pinning; `npm ci` installs the exact tree pinned in `fleet-monitor/server/package-lock.json` (present, 80 KB) and fails fast on `package.json`↔lockfile drift, giving reproducible production installs. `--omit=dev` is the supported replacement for the deprecated `--production` flag. Tap-native installer (no canonical dev-team mirror; `sync-tap.sh` not involved). TEST_PLAN.md expected-result text updated to match. From XACA-0338 audit finding F-07-001.

- XACA-0680: `ccusage_collector.py` self-recycles when source mtime advances. Adds `_source_changed(baseline)` helper and a mtime check at the top of each `run_loop()` poll cycle; on mtime advance the daemon exits cleanly so `_ensure_collector_running()` respawns with updated code. Fixes stale-schema cache after `aiteamforge upgrade` (XACA-0679 by_model all-zero incident). Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/ccusage_collector.py` via `sync-tap.sh`.

- XACA-0655: Comprehensive `aiteamforge doctor` hardening + first-launch self-heal preflight (`libexec/commands/aiteamforge-doctor.sh`, `libexec/commands/aiteamforge-start.sh`; tap-native files — the doctor lives only in the tap, no canonical dev-team mirror, `sync-tap.sh` not involved). Targets the consumer-only failure modes that brick first use and that prior checks missed. **(001) New `check_board_resolution` + `--check board`:** resolves the active team's kanban dir via the SAME runtime resolvers (`aiteamforge_team_kanban_dir` / `get_kanban_dir` / `get_board_id`), then PASSes only when a real `*-board.json` exists, parses as valid JSON (via `${AITEAMFORGE_PYTHON}`, jq fallback), and has non-empty `team`/`backlog` structure. The key new value is **template/stub-collision detection**: it mirrors the runtime placeholder signal (`_aiteamforge_org_slug` == `example-org` → "configured `setup` never ran") and FAILs when board resolution would return a template stub that could mask/collide with a real team board — with the exact `aiteamforge setup` fix. Empty/placeholder boards WARN; unparseable boards FAIL. **(002a) Real iterm2 importability:** `check_python_venv` keeps the existing `pip show` checks but now also runs an actual `${AITEAMFORGE_PYTHON} -c "import iterm2"` — pip-show passing is not proof the module imports (broken/partial venv, wrong interpreter). FAILs with `brew postinstall aiteamforge` when the import fails despite pip-show passing. **(002b) LCARS server durability:** `check_services` now goes beyond reachability — it WARNs (with the `launchctl load …lcars-health.plist` fix) when the server is up but the keepalive/auto-restart LaunchAgent (`com.aiteamforge.lcars-health`) is not loaded (it will die on the next crash and not come back), and flags `.lcars-port` ↔ live-port disagreement. **(003) Real `--fix` remediation:** the bottom-of-file "not yet implemented" stub is replaced by a reusable `attempt_remediation <kind> [--apply]` engine. SAFE/idempotent kinds (venv → `brew postinstall`, keepalive → `launchctl load`, server → `aiteamforge start kanban`) are auto-applied inline at each failing check under `--fix`; RISKY kinds (config/board → `aiteamforge setup`, which rewrites config) are PRINT-ONLY and never auto-run. Every action is logged to a summary. **(004) First-launch preflight self-heal:** new `aiteamforge doctor --preflight` runs only the fast/critical subset (venv+iterm2 import, board resolution, server durability), auto-applies SAFE remediations SILENTLY, logs one line per action to `~/.aiteamforge/logs/doctor-preflight.log`, prints at most a concise one-line summary, surfaces any RISKY/manual items, and NEVER blocks (always exits 0). `aiteamforge start` invokes it via a version-stamped, run-once sentinel (`~/.aiteamforge/.doctor-preflight-<version>`) so a fresh install or `brew upgrade` re-runs it exactly once, wrapped in `|| true` so it can never abort launch. **(006)** doctor `usage()` Components list updated for the new `board` component and the enhanced `python-venv`/`services` checks.

- XACA-0654: `tests/test-fresh-install.sh` now checks the **tap-owned** Python venv (`$HOMEBREW_PREFIX/var/aiteamforge/venv`, resolved via `var/aiteamforge/env.sh`/`AITEAMFORGE_PYTHON`) instead of the deprecated per-install `$INSTALL_DIR/.venv`. The old path no longer exists on current installs, so the test was blind to a missing `iterm2` package in the venv that actually runs the iTerm2 window manager — it now imports `iterm2` against the real venv and fails loudly when absent (and warns if a stale `$INSTALL_DIR/.venv` lingers). Adds an opt-in `--runtime` smoke test that launches a real LCARS server and asserts (1) `aiteamforge start lcars` exits 0, (2) the server is reachable on `/api/status` reporting a configured team, and (3) the server.py process is detached (PPID 1, nohup+disown contract) so it survives shell logout — closing the "green static checks, dead runtime" gap. Default runs stay read-only; cleanup only stops a server when nothing was running beforehand.

- XACA-0679: LCARS Usage Monitor gains a per-model (Opus / Sonnet / Haiku / Fable / Other) token + cost breakdown. Mirrors the canonical `lcars-ui/` changes (`ccusage_collector.py`, `server.py`, `index.html`, `css/usage-indicator.css`) into `share/lcars-ui/`. The collector adds a new top-level `by_model` cache field (cache schema v3→v4) built from `ccusage daily --json --breakdown`, with today + last-7d tokens/cost per tier and last-known-good preservation on ccusage failure; `GET /api/usage/current` passes it through (zero-filled sentinel on error/v3-cache paths); the cockpit renders a "BY MODEL" section that visually flags the 2× Fable escalation tier. Supports the Fable-5 escalation-only policy in `MODEL_SELECTION.md`.

- XACA-0299: Installer-simulation regression test for CR-lifecycle artifact deployment (`tests/test-xaca-0299-cr-schema.sh`). Guards that `install_lcars_profile_script()` correctly deploys `kb-cr.sh` and `migrate-cr-schema.py` to `$AITEAMFORGE_DIR/scripts/` with exec bits set, that the DynamicProfiles write is sandboxed and never touches real `$HOME`, and that the deployed migrator honours its dry-run (exit 0, `[DRY RUN]`, board unchanged, no backup) and no-op (batch v2 board, per-item already-v2, not-found exit 5, no-CR-data exit 6) contracts. Deferred from XACA-0291 PR #326 review subitem XACA-0291-017; EPIC-0017 Phase 1 follow-up.

- XACA-0676: Homebrew untrusted-tap gate was silently breaking aiteamforge auto-upgrade fleet-wide. Recent Homebrew refuses to load formulae from a third-party tap when `$HOMEBREW_REQUIRE_TAP_TRUST` is set (`Error: Refusing to load formula … from untrusted tap`); on a gated box `brew outdated`/`brew upgrade` returned empty/no-op, so `aiteamforge upgrade` printed "Homebrew formula is up to date" and the auto-upgrade LaunchAgent logged a clean no-op while the machine rotted on the old version (observed: M4Mini stuck at 0.13.4 accumulating stale-cockpit bugs). **Fix (three parts):** (1) **Trust on install/setup** — `bin/aiteamforge-setup.sh` runs idempotent `brew trust --tap doublenode/aiteamforge` after sourcing common.sh (only when brew + the tap are present); README + docs/INSTALLATION.md gain an explicit "Step 1b: Trust the tap" since a gated box refuses `brew install` itself before setup ever runs. (2) **Trust on upgrade** — `aiteamforge upgrade` (`check_brew_updates`) and the auto-upgrade LaunchAgent (`share/scripts/auto-upgrade.sh`, mirror of canonical `scripts/auto-upgrade.sh`) call trust BEFORE any brew load. (3) **Detect + surface loudly** — new shared helpers `ensure_tap_trusted` / `tap_load_refused` in `libexec/lib/common.sh` (tap name centralized as `AITF_TAP_NAME_DEFAULT` in `libexec/lib/constants.sh`); `aiteamforge upgrade` now emits a loud WARNING + remediation and returns instead of faking "up to date" when the formula is still refused; the auto-upgrade LaunchAgent exits non-zero with an operator notification; `aiteamforge doctor` gains a `tap-trust` check (run in `all` + standalone `--check tap-trust`) that FAILs loudly with `brew trust --tap doublenode/aiteamforge` remediation. Doctor is detection-only (warn loud, no silent state mutation); install/upgrade auto-trust per operator choice.
- XACA-0470: Per-team Change Request (CR) workflow opt-in + Atlassian credential capture in the setup wizard (`bin/aiteamforge-setup.sh`, `libexec/installers/install-kanban.sh`; tap-native files — no canonical dev-team mirror, `sync-tap.sh` not involved). Previously the CR Confluence poller LaunchAgent was wired to install per-team but the installer never created `~/.config/aiteamforge/confluence-credentials.json`, so on a fresh install the poller was never set up (it silently skipped), and enabling CRs required hand-authoring the creds file. **New UX:** STEP 3.5 of the wizard asks, per selected team, "Enable Change Request workflow for team '<X>'?" (default No); if any team opts in it prompts once for shared Atlassian credentials (email, API token [hidden input, with the id.atlassian.com token-creation link], Confluence site [default `mainevent.atlassian.net`], space key [default `DPD2`]). **Two new config files** in `~/.config/aiteamforge/`: `cr-config.json` (mode 644, non-secret) is the persisted per-team opt-in record (`{version,prompted,teams:{<team>:bool}}`) — it lives outside the install dir so `aiteamforge upgrade` never touches it, and its `prompted` flag suppresses re-nagging; `confluence-credentials.json` (mode 600, secret, written under `umask 077`) holds the shared creds with one entry per opted-in team only. Both writers are merge-safe (a partial re-run never drops other teams' flags/creds). `install_cr_confluence_poller_launchagent` now treats `cr-config.json` as the authority: it renders+loads a plist only for ENABLED teams that have credentials, and `_cr_reconcile_disabled_plists` unloads+removes plists for non-enabled teams — fixing the idle-daemon launchd pollution that motivated the ticket. **Migration (decision c, prompt-at-upgrade):** `maybe_migrate_cr_config` populates `cr-config.json` for pre-flag installs — infer-from-existing-credentials silently when creds already exist, prompt-per-team interactively otherwise, and a no-op (never blocks) under `--non-interactive`/CI/auto-upgrade. New regression test `tests/test-xaca-0470-cr-enable.sh`.

- XACA-0677: `update_runtime_helpers` in `libexec/commands/aiteamforge-upgrade.sh` now also refreshes the root-level `iterm2_window_manager.py` copy (`${WORKING_DIR}/iterm2_window_manager.py`). `open_lcars_tab` in `lcars-launch-helpers.sh` invokes the root copy directly; `install_iterm2_window_manager()` lays it down via plain `cp` at install time. The `update_runtime_helpers` scripts/ sweep never touched this root copy, leaving upgraded boxes running a stale version (e.g. missing the XACA-0652 venv-bootstrap content). Fix: a tightly-scoped block at the end of `update_runtime_helpers` copies `share/scripts/iterm2_window_manager.py` → `${WORKING_DIR}/iterm2_window_manager.py` with `chmod +x`, using plain `cp` (matching the install path — no `_xaca0608_render_team_script` path rewrite, as the file self-locates at runtime via `os.path.dirname(__file__)`). XACA-0673 convention respected: refresh only when the root copy already exists on disk (upgrade never materialises absent files). Dry-run aware. Tap-native file — no canonical dev-team mirror; sync-tap.sh not involved.

- XACA-0469: cr-confluence-poller Pass 2 (`scan_team_approval`) now silent-skips IT Connect `cr_proper_url` values (one-stage CRs — `itconnect.daveandbusters.com`) instead of repeatedly logging "Cannot extract page ID from cr_proper_url" every poll cycle. After XACA-0465, one-stage CRs write an IT Connect ticket URL into `cr_proper_url`; those URLs don't match the Confluence page-ID regex, so the approval-signal scanner failed noisily and never auto-transitioned. **Phase 1 fix:** a primary guard in `scan_team_approval` matches the existing `ITCONNECT_URL_PATTERN` (introduced XACA-0465), emits one clear `log()` line ("itconnect approval-signal scan not implemented; manual approval required"), and `continue`s before any Confluence fetch; a defense-in-depth guard in `fetch_cr_proper_page_data` returns `None` before page-ID extraction for any future caller. Two-stage CRs with a Confluence `cr_proper_url` are unaffected. Approval-signal scanning over IT Connect is deferred to Phase 2 (XACA-0678, gated on IT Connect API access); until then one-stage CRs require manual approval. Mirrors canonical `scripts/cr-confluence-poller.py` via `sync-tap.sh`.

## [0.14.0] - 2026-06-11

- XACA-0664: Aligned the 3 remaining LaunchAgent installers (`install_cellar_watch_launchagent`, `install_cr_confluence_poller_launchagent`, `install_auto_upgrade_launchagent`) in `libexec/installers/install-kanban.sh` to the XACA-0651-009 load-verify pattern — verify registration via `launchctl list` instead of trusting the `launchctl load` exit code (which returns 0 even when launchd rejects the job). Closes a pre-existing k501 sibling-drift gap: XACA-0651-009 previously aligned install_backup, install_lcars_health, install_lcars_watch, and install_lcars_runatload, but missed these three. Tap-native file — no canonical dev-team mirror; sync-tap.sh not involved. Regression test (`tests/test-xaca-0651-consumer-doctor-gaps.sh`) extended with 3 new static-source assertions (Fix 3e, one per function).

- XACA-0671: Reconcile share/personas/<team>/agents to canonical .claude/agents-master/ (full-file mirror, all 11 teams) + NEW dns/mainevent persona dirs. Realigns stale name: frontmatter that mis-bound @claude_agent on tap machines (EMH medical→emh, Nahla chancellor→nahla, Reno engineering→reno, etc.), stale medical descriptions, and body drift. Going forward sync-tap.sh enforces this mirror (drift gate). avatars/ + prompts/ for dns/mainevent remain absent (separate asset pipeline — follow-up).

- XACA-0672: Extract a shared `resolve_lcars_port_fallback <cksum_input> <base> <range>` helper in `share/scripts/lcars-launch-helpers.sh` (mirror of canonical `scripts/lcars-launch-helpers.sh` via `sync-tap.sh`). The legacy cksum-band fallback `8180 + $(echo "dns-framework" | cksum | cut -d' ' -f1) % 20` was hand-duplicated across three dev-team-only DNS scripts (`dns-startup.sh` / `dns-connect.sh` / `dns-disconnect.sh`, not tap-mirrored), each with a comment begging editors to keep the copies byte-identical — a k501 sibling-drift hazard. The helper echoes `base + (cksum(input) % range)` and deliberately preserves the `echo "$input"` (trailing-newline) form so the CRC — and therefore the DNS port (8180) — is byte-for-byte unchanged; dropping the newline (`printf '%s'`) would shift it to 8192. Helper-only addition to the tap copy; no behaviour change for tap consumers (the three DNS callers are dev-team-only).

- XACA-0673: Consumer LCARS cockpit web tab broken after UPGRADE — `update_runtime_helpers` now materialises mandatory shared modules even when the working-dir target is absent (`libexec/commands/aiteamforge-upgrade.sh`; tap-native, no canonical mirror). XACA-0608's runtime-helper sweep refreshes ONLY scripts a machine already installed (`[ -f "$target" ] || continue`) — correct for optional/layout-specific helpers, but fatal for a BRAND-NEW shared module imported by an ALREADY-installed script. A machine that auto-upgraded across XACA-0652 (which added `iterm2_venv_bootstrap.py`, imported by `iterm-browser.py` / `iterm2_window_manager.py`) had its importer refreshed in place — gaining the new `import iterm2_venv_bootstrap` line — but the module itself was skipped (absent target), so `import iterm2` failed and the LCARS web cockpit tab never created (observed on M4Mini at 0.13.4; the install path already seeds it, so only upgraded machines were affected). **Fix:** new `_xaca0673_mandatory_materialize_basenames()` set (currently `iterm2_venv_bootstrap.py`); the absent-target skip is bypassed for members of this set so they are created on upgrade. Optional helpers are still skipped when absent (XACA-0608 behaviour preserved; its 21-test suite stays green). New regression test `tests/test-xaca-0673-mandatory-materialize.sh` (6 cases) including an anti-drift PARITY GUARD that fails if any `share/scripts/*.py` module imported by another shipped script is missing from the mandatory set.

- XACA-0632: Ship the `kb-variance` estimate-vs-actual handicap reporter to installed-tap users by porting it into the kanban aliases template (`share/templates/aliases/kanban-aliases.sh`). The CLI previously lived only in dev `kanban-helpers.sh` (not tap-mirrored), so tap users could reach variance analytics through the LCARS panel but not the command line. The ported function's jq payload and human table are byte-identical to the canonical dev implementation — the only tap-side adaptations are the helper calls (`_kb_check_jq` for jq availability and `_kb_get_team`/`_kb_get_board_file` for board resolution, in place of the dev `_kb_ensure_jq`/`_kb_detect_context`); the `--json` payload guarded by `test_xaca0630_parity.py` is unchanged. `kb-help` now lists `kb-variance` under a new "Reporting / Analytics" section. A tap-native drift-guard test (`tests/test-xaca-0632-kb-variance.sh`) pins the shipped contract and, on a dev checkout, asserts the tap copy's `--json` stays byte-identical to the dev canonical. Follow-up from XACA-0630-015.

- XACA-0661: Harden `start_lcars_server` against stale-log masquerade and concurrent-startup SIGTERM/143 race (`share/scripts/lcars-launch-helpers.sh`; mirror of canonical `scripts/lcars-launch-helpers.sh` via `sync-tap.sh`). **(006)** The per-team server log was appended (`2>>`) across restarts and only size-capped, so the failure-path `tail` calls resurfaced historical FATALs from prior incidents and made them look like the current failure; the log is now rolled to a single `.old` backup at the top of every invocation so each tail shows only current-start stderr. **(005)** Failure-path messages now decode the exit status honestly (143 → SIGTERM/concurrent re-entrancy, 129 → SIGHUP/nohup regression, other → crash), and the stale-server `pkill` pattern is anchored at a port word boundary to avoid numeric-prefix false matches. **(007)** A per-port advisory lock (atomic `mkdir /tmp/lcars-start-lock-<PORT>`, portable across bash 3.2 and zsh) serializes concurrent `start_lcars_server` calls so a second invocation can no longer SIGTERM the first call's freshly-launched server; includes dead-PID + 30s-age stale-lock reclaim, a healthy-server short-circuit, and explicit lock release on all five exit paths (`trap … RETURN` is a bash-only extension, unavailable in zsh). **(008)** the 30s age-guard reads the lockdir mtime via GNU `stat -c %Y` first, then BSD `stat -f %m`, then `echo 0` — GNU-first because BSD `stat -f %m` does not error on Linux (it exits 0 with filesystem data, not mtime), so the age-guard now works on Linux as well as macOS. No change to the durable nohup/disown launch (XACA-0652), the readiness poll, `open_lcars_tab`, or `resolve_lcars_port`.

- XACA-0667: Tap-side persona delivery for legal/finance/medical nested project dirs (`share/scripts/deploy-worktree-personas.sh`, `share/scripts/lcars-launch-helpers.sh`; mirrors of canonical `scripts/deploy-worktree-personas.sh` + `scripts/lcars-launch-helpers.sh` via `sync-tap.sh`). On a tap-consumer box `deploy_team_personas()` was a silent no-op because `kb-sync-personas` (dev-only, not tap-mirrored) is absent and the `[ -x ]` guard short-circuited, leaving `~/<team>/<PROJECTID>/.claude/agents/` empty. **(002) `deploy-worktree-personas.sh`** gains: `_write_exclude` helper (idempotent `.git/info/exclude` update via `git rev-parse --git-common-dir`, mirroring the XACA-0660 kb-sync-personas pattern); `_deploy_nested_main_root` function (validates project_dir is a git repo root and not a linked worktree, writes exclude, deploys via `_deploy_core`); `--nested-main-root <project_dir> <team>` CLI mode in `main()`. NOTE comment in `_deploy_all` updated to reflect that the gap is now closed. **(003) `lcars-launch-helpers.sh` `deploy_team_personas()`** gains a tap-consumer fallback branch: when `kb-sync-personas` is absent it invokes `deploy-worktree-personas.sh --nested-main-root "$project_dir" "$team"` (deploying from `${AITEAMFORGE_DIR}/<team>/personas/agents/`) with the same non-fatal `|| echo` guard. `deploy_team_personas()` takes the project dir as an explicit 3rd argument — the `legal`/`finance`/`medical` startup scripts now pass `"$PROJECT_DIR"`; it falls back to `$PWD` only when omitted. (QA-caught: startup scripts run from a different cwd than the project root, so a bare `$PWD` would have deployed into the wrong directory.) Dev-machine path (kb-sync-personas present) is unchanged.

- XACA-0626: LCARS port-drift self-heal + reboot-durable auto-restart on tap machines (`libexec/installers/install-kanban.sh`, `libexec/installers/install-team.sh`, `libexec/commands/aiteamforge-upgrade.sh`, `libexec/commands/aiteamforge-uninstall.sh`, `libexec/commands/aiteamforge-migrate.sh`, NEW `share/templates/auto-upgrade/lcars-runatload.template.plist`, `share/scripts/lcars-health-check.sh`, `share/scripts/kb-port-reconcile` (NEW mirror), `share/scripts/lcars-launch-helpers.sh`, `share/templates/team-startup.sh.template`, `share/templates/team-project-startup.sh.template`; mirrors of canonical dev-team changes via `sync-tap.sh`). The 2026-06-03 incident left finance-personal LCARS unreachable on a tap machine because port drift could not self-heal there. **(001) Ship `kb-port-reconcile`:** `install-kanban.sh::install_kb_port_reconcile_script` seeds it to `$AITEAMFORGE_DIR/scripts/`; `aiteamforge-upgrade.sh` aux-script-map refreshes it on upgrade; new `--prefer canonical` mode treats `aiteamforge_paths.py` DEFAULT_TEAMS as sole authority (for when `team-paths.json` is the drifted source). **(002) Startup drift guard:** `start_lcars_server()` self-heals a stale `lcars-ports/<session>.port` and warns on team-paths divergence — runs on every launch via the shared chokepoint (layered on XACA-0614's `resolve_lcars_python`/headless refactor). **(007) Reboot durability:** `lcars-health-check.sh` Defect-B path fix (derive from `AITEAMFORGE_DIR`, not hardcoded `~/dev-team`; two-dir probe so dev-machine runs resolve too) + Defect-C gate relax (restart configured teams regardless of post-reboot tmux/process state); NEW installer-rendered `com.aiteamforge.lcars-runatload` LaunchAgent (`RunAtLoad`, fires `aiteamforge start lcars` at login/reboot) with the full XACA-0578 5-site wiring and the XACA-0651-009 load-verify pattern (verify via `launchctl list`, not the `launchctl load` exit code). **(008) Self-collision fix:** `install-team.sh` reuses an instance's existing team-paths port on re-install instead of reallocating (root cause of the +1 drift). All durability delivered through the install/setup/upgrade flow only — no hand-rolled plists (XACA-0626-005 constraint).

- XACA-0666: Auto-deploy personas into multi-project personal-team project dirs at startup (`share/scripts/lcars-launch-helpers.sh`, `share/scripts/teams/legal-startup.sh`, `share/scripts/teams/finance-startup.sh`, `share/scripts/teams/medical-startup.sh`; mirror of the canonical root `<team>-startup.sh` + `scripts/lcars-launch-helpers.sh`). The legal/finance/medical teams `cd` into `~/<team>/<PROJECTID>` (a nested git repo), but their persona manifest target is the umbrella parent (`~/<team>`), so personas were deployed one level above the project git root — where Claude Code never looks — and sessions loaded zero personas. A new shared helper `deploy_team_personas <team> <label>` in `lcars-launch-helpers.sh` (already sourced by every startup script) runs a guarded, non-fatal `kb-sync-personas sync-worktrees <team>`; each of the three `*-startup.sh` calls it immediately after `kb_ensure_team_initialized`, invoking the existing XACA-0660 nested-root deploy so the active project dir's `.claude/agents` is populated/refreshed at launch (kept untracked via `.git/info/exclude`). Single shared implementation (one helper, three callers) avoids k501 sibling-drift. Covers new projects and master-persona drift automatically. The guard (`[ -x ... ]` + `|| echo`) makes it a safe no-op where `kb-sync-personas` is absent (it is dev-team-only, not tap-mirrored). Mirror of canonical changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0614: Headless/remote startup for AITeamForge teams (`share/scripts/lcars-launch-helpers.sh`, `share/scripts/lcars-health-check.sh`, `share/scripts/teams/finance-startup.sh`, `share/scripts/teams/freelance-startup.sh`, `share/scripts/teams/legal-startup.sh`, `share/scripts/teams/medical-startup.sh`; plus the 7 non-parametric startup scripts — dev-team only, not tap-mirrored). `lcars-launch-helpers.sh` gains three new helpers: `is_headless()` (detects SSH/headless environments by checking for absence of display and GUI), `has_iterm_gui()` (wraps the previous iTerm2 TERM_PROGRAM/pgrep test), and `resolve_lcars_python()` (abstracts Python resolution for tap-installed vs dev-source machines); `start_lcars_server` now calls `resolve_lcars_python()` instead of hardcoding `python3`. All 11 startup scripts replace 44 literal `TERM_PROGRAM/pgrep iTerm.app` tests with `has_iterm_gui()` and convert the 2-way GUI gate to a 3-way branch (iTerm2 full cockpit / Terminal.app fallback / headless print-only). `start_lcars_server` is moved unconditional so the LCARS server boots on headless hosts too. `lcars-health-check.sh` (`_hc_start_lcars_server`) sources `lcars-launch-helpers.sh` at runtime and delegates to `resolve_lcars_python()`; eliminates its own duplicate Python-resolution chain while preserving XACA-0652's HUP-immune nohup+disown launch form. The 4 parametric connect scripts (finance/freelance/legal/medical) gain auto-start logic: idempotency guard (no-op if team already up), cold-vs-half-up detection, and a 15×3 s poll loop — so `<team>-connect.sh <host>` can be called immediately on a freshly-booted remote without waiting for the operator to start the remote stack manually. Mirror of canonical `scripts/lcars-launch-helpers.sh`, `lcars-health-check.sh`, and `scripts/teams/<team>-startup.sh` changes via `sync-tap.sh`.

- XACA-0651: Consumer `aiteamforge doctor` + fresh-install gaps (both tap-native, no canonical mirror). **Fix 1 — false `LCARS health check script missing` FAILURE (`libexec/commands/aiteamforge-doctor.sh`, `check_launchagents`):** the script-present check referenced bare `${AITEAMFORGE_DIR}`, which is unset in the doctor context on consumers, so the path collapsed to `/lcars-health-check.sh` (root) and reported a FAILURE even when the script was correctly installed at `${working_dir}/lcars-health-check.sh`. Now resolves the path via `get_working_dir` like its six sibling checks (`AITEAMFORGE_DIR:-$HOME/aiteamforge`). **Fix 2 — spurious `Dev-team not a git repository` warning (`check_git`):** a consumer tap install lays `${working_dir}` (e.g. `~/aiteamforge`) down as a plain product dir with no `.git` — expected and healthy, not a fault. The check was dev-source-of-truth-centric and warned on every consumer; now it gates the clean/dirty checks on `.git` presence and emits an informational `print_info` note on consumers instead of a warning. **Fix 3 — kanban-backup LaunchAgent not loaded on fresh install (`libexec/installers/install-kanban.sh`, `install_backup_launchagent`):** the agent is `RunAtLoad=true` and redirects stdout/stderr into `~/aiteamforge-backups/kanban/` — a directory only created by `install_kanban_backup`, which early-returns when the backup script source is missing — so launchd could not open the job's StandardOutPath and the agent never registered (the lcars-health agent logs to `/tmp` and always loads, the exact asymmetry observed). Now: (a) `mkdir -p` the log dir in the loader so the load is self-sufficient; (b) guard the `{{BACKUP_INTERVAL}}` substitution against an empty/non-numeric value that would render an invalid `<integer></integer>` plist, falling back to the documented `900`s default; (c) verify registration via `launchctl list` after load rather than trusting the legacy `launchctl load` exit code (which returns 0 even on reject), surfacing an accurate warning + manual-load hint on failure. **Sibling-drift alignment (XACA-0651-009, PR #559 review):** `install_lcars_health_launchagent` and `install_lcars_watch_launchagent` shared the same blind `if launchctl load; then` success-trust pattern; both now use `launchctl load || true` + a `launchctl list` registration check so neither inherits the false-success bug if it ever gains a non-`/tmp` log path or RunAtLoad behavior. Regression test `tests/test-xaca-0651-consumer-doctor-gaps.sh` (22 cases, incl. sibling-drift anti-regression).

- XACA-0609: Lowercase `PROJECTID` for legal-startup session names (`share/scripts/teams/legal-startup.sh`, mirror of canonical root `legal-startup.sh`). `legal-startup.sh` built tmux session names from the raw `$PROJECTID`, so they only matched the canonical instance id `legal-coparenting` by accident when the id was already lowercase. Added `PROJECT_LOWER=$(echo "$PROJECTID" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')` + `SESSION_PREFIX="legal-${PROJECT_LOWER}"` and routed all session-name uses (7 `terminals[]` keys, `terminal_order[]`, `session_name=`, both `start_lcars_server` args, `lcars-target.js` append, `open_lcars_tab` arg, `resolve_lcars_port`) through `${SESSION_PREFIX}` — parity with `finance-startup.sh`/`medical-startup.sh`. Also unified the LCARS port fallback to hash `${PROJECT_LOWER}` instead of the constant string `"legal"` (distinct legal projects now get distinct fallback ports); legal's own port band `8220 + cksum % 20` is unchanged. Raw `PROJECTID` preserved for the directory path, exported env vars, and log filenames. Mirror of canonical root `legal-startup.sh` via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0658: Versioned release-push flow — config-driven version bump + verify gate + RELNOTES promotion + branch-push auto-promotion (`share/scripts/kb-release-version-bump`, `share/scripts/kb-release-version-gate`, `share/scripts/kb-release-push-promote`, `share/scripts/lib/kb-relnotes-promote.sh` [all NEW], `share/kanban-hooks/aiteamforge_paths.py`, `share/lcars-ui/server.py`, `share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-busters `lcars.js?v=3.52`→`?v=3.53`, `lcars.css?v=32.22`→`?v=32.23`). A generic, per-team-configurable release-push pipeline. **Config (optional `team-paths.json` keys, backward-compatible):** `version_sources[]` (`{platform, file, match_pattern, write_pattern, encoding, replace_all}`), `relnotes_sources[]` (`{platform, dir}`), and `branch_env_map` (branch→env; accepts a string `"develop":"DEV"` = all release platforms OR a per-platform object `"develop":{"ios":"DEV"}`). New `aiteamforge_paths.py` accessors `get_team_version_sources`/`get_team_branch_env_map`/`get_team_relnotes_sources`/`normalize_branch_env`. **Auto-bump** (`kb-release-version-bump`): writes a release's `platformVersions.<plat>` target into the configured file(s); honors `replace_all`; idempotent; introduces a generic plain-text `VERSION` convention for platforms lacking a native version file. **Verify gate** (`kb-release-version-gate`): hard-blocks (exit 1) on code-version ≠ release-target; non-PROD + unset target = warn, PROD + unset target = FAIL; persists `platforms.<plat>.gateStatus` to the board via `POST /api/releases/<id>/platform-gate-status` (mirrors through XACA-0659's `_sync_release_metadata_to_manifest`), rendered read-only as a PASS/FAIL/SKIP badge on the LCARS release platform card with a >24h stale marker. **RELNOTES** (`lib/kb-relnotes-promote.sh`, sourceable): copies + header-rewrites RELNOTES between envs, backs up an existing destination, lays down the store-asset STUB at PROD (no LLM copy from a shell context), auto-commits non-fatally. **Branch-push auto-promotion** (`kb-release-push-promote` + `kb-git-push` wrapper): resolves branch→env, disambiguates multiple active releases, runs the gate as a hard block, promotes via the `/promote` endpoint, then promotes RELNOTES; PROD advances stage + stub but never auto-generates store copy (prints a loud manual `/relnotes` + `/cr` prompt); LCARS-down and already-at-env are non-fatal no-ops. 115 tests (86 pytest + 29 zsh). Mirror of canonical `scripts/`, `kanban-hooks/`, `lcars-ui/` changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0657: Bidirectional CR ↔ Release assignment + suppress create-CR prompt when linked (`share/scripts/kb-cr.sh`, `share/lcars-ui/server.py`, `share/lcars-ui/js/lcars.js`, `share/lcars-ui/js/lcars-cr-tab.js`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars.css`, `share/lcars-ui/css/lcars-cr-tab.css`, `share/lcars-ui/index.html`; cache-busters `lcars.js?v=3.51`→`?v=3.52`, `lcars-cr-tab.js`→`?v=1.1`, `lcars.css?v=32.21`→`?v=32.22`, `lcars-cr-tab.css`→`?v=1.1`). Adds a bidirectional link between Change Requests and Releases. **Data model:** a CR record gains `releaseAssignment {releaseId, releaseName, assignedAt}`; a release object gains `linkedCRs [{crId, crTitle, linkedAt}]`; the release manifest gains `crIds[]`. Absent = unlinked (no mass migration; readers default cleanly). **Helpers (`kb-cr assign-release`/`unassign-release`, `kb-release link-cr`/`unlink-cr`):** all four entry points delegate to one shared `_kb_cr_release_link`/`_kb_cr_release_unlink` pair (sibling-drift prevention) that writes all three sites; title/name snapshots captured at link time; re-link unlinks the old release first; idempotent. **Server API:** `POST /api/releases/<id>/crs` (body `{crId}`) and `DELETE /api/releases/<id>/crs/<crId>` with CR/release existence validation and an OPTIONAL platform match (non-blocking warning in the response, link still proceeds); the endpoint writes byte-identical board structure to the shell helpers (verified by a parity diff in testing — which caught and fixed a manifest-path divergence: flat `<id>.json` vs the canonical `<id>/manifest.json` subdir, plus two `updatedAt` stamp mismatches). **Release Manager skill:** the PROD-promote step now inspects `linkedCRs` and skips the `/cr` create suggestion when a CR is already linked (prose + workflow example kept consistent). **LCARS UI:** the CR tab row shows its linked Release (clickable → Releases section) and the release card shows linked CR chips (clickable → CHANGE REQ section), reusing the existing `switchSection` + highlight-pulse nav. Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/` + `scripts/kb-cr.sh` changes via `sync-tap.sh`.
- XACA-0660: k501 sibling-drift NOTE in `share/scripts/deploy-worktree-personas.sh` (comment-only mirror of canonical `scripts/deploy-worktree-personas.sh`). dev-team's `kb-sync-personas sync-worktrees` gained a loop that also deploys personas into the nested main git worktree (e.g. `<container>/develop/`) for container-layout teams (Firebase/Android). That fix is intentionally NOT replicated in this script — it deploys from the tap installation path (`~/aiteamforge/.../personas/agents/`), unavailable on dev-source machines, so it cannot read the dev-team master the nested-main-root deploy requires. On tap machines `kb-sync-personas sync-worktrees` is authoritative for nested-main-root deployment. A NOTE comment documents this at the gap site. No functional change.
- XACA-0659: Release `manifest.json` write-through on update + promote (`share/lcars-ui/server.py`, mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/server.py`). `handle_update_release` and `handle_promote_release` previously wrote release changes only to the authoritative board `releases[]`, leaving each release's per-release `manifest.json` carrying permanently stale top-level scalars (`name`, `version`, `versionCode`, `currentEnvironment`). New helper `_sync_release_metadata_to_manifest` mirrors board-authoritative values into the manifest after every save — top-level metadata (`name`/`shortTitle`/`type`/`status`/`targetDate`), the full `platforms` object, and legacy flat scalars (`version`/`versionCode`=buildNumber/`currentEnvironment`) derived from a representative platform (single platform → that platform; multi-platform → first key alphabetically for determinism). A `_source` marker stamps the file as a derived mirror. Manifest-sync failures are logged and never abort the board write. New test suite `share/lcars-ui/tests/test_xaca0659_release_metadata_sync.py` (33 tests). Mirror of canonical `lcars-ui/` changes via `sync-tap.sh`. (CLI-side `kb-release promote` verb + `--platform-build` flag ship in dev-team's non-tap `kanban-helpers.sh`.)

## [0.13.4] - 2026-06-09

- XACA-0652: Durable LCARS server launch + shared iterm2 venv bootstrap for cockpit scripts. **Durable server launch (`share/scripts/lcars-launch-helpers.sh`):** `start_lcars_server` now launches `server.py` via `nohup env ... sh -c '... exec python3 ...'` with `disown`, ensuring the server survives the startup script's exit on tap-installed machines (e.g. M4Mini) where the parent shell exits and would previously SIGHUP the server process group. `exec` inside `sh -c` replaces `sh` so `$!` remains the python PID. Readiness check hardened: after the first `/api/status 200`, a 0.3s pause + re-check confirms persistence. **Shared iterm2 bootstrap (`share/scripts/iterm2_venv_bootstrap.py`, NEW):** the tap-venv probe/re-exec logic from XACA-0650's `iterm2_window_manager.py` is extracted into a standalone module added to the `sync-tap.sh` mirror map and deployed by both the parametric and rendered-template install paths in `libexec/installers/install-team.sh`. **Sibling cockpit scripts fixed (`share/lcars-ui/iterm-browser.py`, `share/scripts/iterm2_window_manager.py`):** both now import the shared bootstrap module instead of inline probes. `set-lcars-profile-browser.py` and `create-agent-panel-profile.py` are unchanged (they do not import iterm2). `iterm2_window_manager.py` uses a two-candidate path search (same-dir for tap layout, scripts/ subdir for dev-source layout). `iterm-browser.py` uses `../scripts` which resolves correctly in both layouts. Upgrade auto-coverage: `update_runtime_helpers` already sweeps `share/scripts/*.py` so the bootstrap will be refreshed on `brew upgrade` once it is installed. **PR #551 review fix — lazy brew probe:** `ensure_iterm2_venv()` previously ran two `brew --prefix` subprocess calls at module-import time unconditionally. Inverted to fast-path first: `importlib.util.find_spec("iterm2")` is checked at the top of `ensure_iterm2_venv()` — if iterm2 is already importable (the common case when running under the tap venv or after re-exec), the function returns immediately with zero subprocess calls. The `brew --prefix` candidate probe is now inside a lazy `_get_tap_venv_candidates()` helper, only called when `find_spec` returns None. The execv infinite-loop guard is implicit: after re-exec the new python3 has iterm2, so fast-path returns before reaching the candidates. Tap mirror synced (`share/scripts/iterm2_venv_bootstrap.py`).

## [0.13.3] - 2026-06-08

- XACA-0650: Fix stale `~/aiteamforge/.venv` remediation message in `iterm2_window_manager.py` and add tap-venv dep check to `aiteamforge doctor`. **Fix 1 (canonical `share/scripts/iterm2_window_manager.py`):** the docstring and the `ImportError` handler both previously told users to provision `~/aiteamforge/.venv` — a path deprecated when XACA-0175 moved Python deps to the tap-owned venv at `$HOMEBREW_PREFIX/var/aiteamforge/venv`. Updated docstring and error messages to point at the correct venv location and instruct `brew postinstall aiteamforge` as the fix. The `ImportError` handler now resolves venv candidates in priority order: `brew --prefix`-derived path → `/opt/homebrew/var/aiteamforge/venv` → `/usr/local/var/aiteamforge/venv` (covers both Apple Silicon and Intel Macs). The deprecated `~/aiteamforge/.venv` and `~/dev-team/.venv` candidates are removed.
